# Graph Report - monkeyhub  (2026-10-26)

## Corpus Check
- 5811 files · ~7,353,630 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 116870 nodes · 162339 edges · 3411 communities (3152 shown, 259 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 97% EXTRACTED · 3% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 4483 edges (avg confidence: 0.8)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `b79960b7`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
